(function () {
	window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];

	// Signup and login succeed on a redirect, so the server queues the event in
	// a short-lived cookie (see helpers/analytics_event_helper.php) and the
	// next page reports it once.
	var queued = document.cookie.match(/(?:^|; )ut_ga_event=([a-z_]+)\.([a-z_]+)(?:;|$)/);
	if (queued) {
		document.cookie = 'ut_ga_event=; path=/; max-age=0';
		window.dataLayer.push({ event: queued[1], method: queued[2] });
	}

	// Matched by app id or our untp.beer short links, so links to other apps,
	// such as Authy, are not counted.
	function reportAppStoreClick(e) {
		// auxclick also fires for a right-click, which opens a menu, not the link.
		if (e.type === 'auxclick' && e.button !== 1) {
			return;
		}

		var link = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('a[href]');
		var app = link && /(id449141888|untp\.beer\/\w*(?:iphone|ios))|com\.untappdllc\.app|untp\.beer\/\w*android/.exec(link.href);
		if (!app) {
			return;
		}

		var store = app[1] ? 'apple' : 'google';

		// The Firebase SDK reads the Play install referrer, so this is how an
		// Android install is credited to the web page that sent it. The App
		// Store has no equivalent without an App Store Connect campaign token.
		var url = new URL(link.href);
		if (url.hostname === 'play.google.com' && !url.searchParams.has('referrer')) {
			var section = location.pathname.split('/')[1] || 'home';
			url.searchParams.set('referrer', 'utm_source=untappd_web&utm_medium=referral&utm_campaign=' + section);
			link.href = url.href;
		}

		window.dataLayer.push({ event: 'app_store_click', store: store, page_path: location.pathname });
	}

	// A middle-click opens the link in a new tab and fires auxclick, not click.
	document.addEventListener('click', reportAppStoreClick, true);
	document.addEventListener('auxclick', reportAppStoreClick, true);
})();
</script>

		{{#if beers}}
			{{#if firstTime}}
				<div class="menu-section-header">
					<h4>Menu Results</h4>
				</div>
				<ul class="menu-section-list">
			{{/if}}

				<li class="sorting-item">
					<div class="beer-info {{handleYourCount this}}">
					    <div class="beer-label">
					    	{{handleNewCondition this}}
					        <img src="{{this.beer.beer_label}}">
					    </div>
					    <div class="beer-details">
					        <h5><a class="track-click" data-track="menu" data-href=":beer" href="/b/{{this.beer.beer_slug}}/{{this.beer.bid}}">{{this.beer.beer_name}}</a> <em>{{this.beer.beer_style}}</em></h5>
							<!-- ABV_EQUAL_ZERO and IBU_EQUAL_ZERO ARE NOT USED HERE. THIS IS HANDLED IN WEB ASSETS -->
					        <h6><span>{{handleABV this}} ABV • {{handleIBU this}} IBU • <a class="track-click" data-track="menu" data-href=":brewery" href="/w/{{this.brewery.brewery_slug}}/{{this.brewery.brewery_id}}">{{this.brewery.brewery_name}}</a> • </span>
					        {{handleBeerRating this}}
					    </div>
					</div>
					{{#if this.containers}}
						{{#handlePriceFormat this}}
							<div class="beer-prices">
								{{#each this.containers}}
									{{#checkPriceFormat this}}
										<p>
									{{else}}
										<div style="font-weight: 600; margin-bottom: 8px;">
									{{/checkPriceFormat}}

									{{#checkContainerFormat this}}
										<span class="size">{{this.serving_type}}</span>
									{{/checkContainerFormat}}

									{{#checkPriceFormat this}}
										<span class="price">
											{{this.price.currency_symbol}}
											{{this.price.value}}
										</span>
									{{/checkPriceFormat}}

									{{#checkPriceFormat this}}
										</p>
									{{else}}
										</div>
									{{/checkPriceFormat}}

								{{/each}}
							</div>
						{{else}}
							{{#checkNonPriceFormat this}}
								<div class="beer-containers">
									<p>
								        <strong>Serving:</strong>
										{{printServingTypes this}}
								    </p>
								</div>
							{{/checkNonPriceFormat}}
						{{/handlePriceFormat}}
					{{/if}}
				</li>
			{{/each}}
			{{#if firstTime}}
				</ul>
			{{/if}}
		{{/if}}
	</script>
